The code for this paper can be found at: \href{https://github.com/hesamaraghi/static-in-frames-dynamic-in-events}{https://github.com/hesamaraghi/static-in-frames-dynamic-in-events}.

</details>


### [105] [Foundation Model-Enabled Efficient Data Sampling (FEEDS): A label-efficient training strategy for pan-cancer, multi-tracer PET/CT datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11076)
*Biratal Raj Wagle,Bashirul Azam Biswas,Grant Chau,Matthew E. Maeder,Muhammad Azeem Arshad,Michael S. Leapman,James B. Yu,Indrani Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FEEDS是一种基于视觉基础模型嵌入的标签与计算高效学习策略，用于全身PET/CT影像中的自动病灶分割。它通过选择最具信息量和多样性的未标注病例进行专家标注，仅需70%的标注数据即可达到全量标注（100%）的训练性能，并能在多种示踪剂和疾病上实现泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身PET/CT病灶分割模型的训练需要大量高质量标注数据，而人工标注既耗时又需要高度专业知识，导致标注数据稀缺。现有模型在有限标注数据下准确性和泛化能力不足，难以满足临床使用需求。

Method: FEEDS利用视觉基础模型（vision foundation model）嵌入来筛选最具信息量和多样性的未标注病例用于专家标注。与无监督、半监督和主动学习不同，FEEDS采用一步式训练范式，仅需一个有限的代表性训练集，从而在标签和计算两方面都实现高效。模型在AutoPET-III数据集上训练，并在AutoPET-III、DeepPSMA和Dartmouth-Hitchcock医学中心三个独立测试集上进行验证。

Result: FEEDS在体素、病灶和解剖区域三个层面均优于随机采样标注、伪标签半监督学习以及仅用有限标注数据训练的方法。它在FDG和PSMA两种示踪剂及多种疾病上均表现出良好的跨数据集泛化能力，用70%更少的标注负担即可匹敌全量标注（100%）的训练性能。

Conclusion: FEEDS有效解决了自动病灶分割中标签稀缺的问题，为从大规模未标注临床数据集中构建代表性且多样化的标注队列提供了实用方案，具有重要的临床应用价值。

Abstract: Automated lesion segmentation in whole-body PET/CT imaging can assist clinicians with cancer detection, staging, and treatment planning across radiotracers and cancer types. However, training lesion segmentation models that capture variations in lesion size, distribution, and appearance requires large annotated datasets, whose creation is both time- and expertise-intensive. As a result, models trained on limited labeled PET/CT data often lack the accuracy and generalizability needed for clinical use. We present FEEDS (Foundation model-Enabled Efficient Data Sampling), a label- and compute-efficient learning strategy that uses vision foundation model embeddings to select the most informative and diverse unlabeled cases for expert annotation. Unlike unsupervised, semi-supervised, and active learning approaches, FEEDS is a one-step training paradigm requiring only a limited, representative training set, making it label- and compute-efficient. We train and validate FEEDS using the AutoPET-III dataset. We test its accuracy and generalizability on three held-out sets: AutoPET-III, DeepPSMA, and an internal Dartmouth-Hitchcock Medical Center dataset. We evaluate clinical utility at the voxel, lesion, and anatomic region level to assess performance in high-risk areas and treatment planning utility. FEEDS outperforms random-sampling-based labeling, pseudolabel-based semi-supervised learning, and training with limited labeled data alone. It generalizes across all three test sets, FDG and PSMA tracers, and multiple diseases, matching fully-labeled (100\%) training performance with 70\% less annotation burden. FEEDS addresses the challenge of label scarcity in an automatic lesion segmentation framework by providing a practical approach for constructing representative and diverse annotation queues from large, unannotated clinical repositories.

</details>


### [106] [Learning Gaussian Structure: Intervention-Guided Density Control for Feed-Forward Driving Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11077)
*Hang Li,Jiahe Li,Meiying Gu,Jin Zheng,Lina Yu,Xiao Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LGS框架，通过学习高斯密度化策略和跨时间点查询机制，在无需优化式训练的情况下增强前馈高斯重建的结构和属性预测，在Waymo和PandaSet数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈LiDAR高斯重建方法将初始化原语集作为最终表示，无法像优化式3DGS那样通过梯度累积实现密度化；同时共享稀疏骨干仅在隐式层面融合多时间戳观测，缺乏对单个原语的跨时间证据显式聚合。

Method: LGS包含两个核心组件：1) Gaussian Densify Policy通过控制剪枝/添加干预产生的局部梯度响应变化，学习一个包含剪枝分数和添加分数的Densify Map，并在推理时直接调整高斯结构；2) Cross-Time Point Query显式检索并聚合其他时间戳高斯原语的邻域特征，以提升属性预测的可靠性。

Result: 在Waymo Open Dataset和PandaSet上的大量实验表明，LGS在重建质量上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式学习高斯结构的动态调整策略以及跨时间的特征聚合，前馈高斯重建能够在无需优化迭代的情况下获得与优化式方法相当的结构自适应能力，验证了干预驱动密度化与跨时间查询的有效性。

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction has recently emerged as an efficient approach for driving scene reconstruction. However, prevailing LiDAR-based methods preserve the initial correspondence between observed points and Gaussian primitives, treating the initialized primitive set as the final representation. Unlike optimization-based 3DGS, these methods cannot accumulate gradients during training to determine how the scenes representation should be densified. Meanwhile, the shared sparse backbone only fuses observations from different timestamps implicitly, without explicitly aggregating cross-time evidence for individual primitives. In this paper, we present Learning Gaussian Structure (LGS), a framework that enhances both Gaussian structure and primitive attributes. Our key observation is that changes in local gradient responses induced by a prune or add intervention reveal whether the corresponding structural adjustment benefits reconstruction. Based on this observation, our Gaussian Densify Policy learns a Densify Map comprising Prune and Addition Scores from controlled interventions, and directly adjusts the Gaussian structure during inference. We further develop a compact Cross-Time Point Query that explicitly retrieves and aggregates neighboring features from Gaussian primitives at other timestamps for reliable attribute prediction. Extensive experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset and PandaSet demonstrate that LGS consistently outperforms existing methods.

</details>


### [107] [Every Packet Counts: Dispersing Information for Loss-Resilient Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11096)
*Yuhang Wei,Chuqin Zhou,Yibo Shi,Jing Wang,Guo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端的抗丢包图像压缩方案，通过通道间重分布（ICR）、交错通道分组（ICG）和双层双分支自回归结构，分别解决信息分布不均、顺序解码依赖和级联错误传播三大问题，在20%丢包率下平均PSNR提升1.84 dB，且能泛化到突发丢包场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式图像压缩（LIC）方法在卫星和应急通信等场景下对数据包丢失非常脆弱，主要源于打包阶段信息分布不均匀以及熵编码阶段顺序解码依赖两大问题。

Method: 1) 打包前引入Inter-Channel Redistribution（ICR）机制重分布通道能量，防止关键信息集中在少数通道；2) 采用Interleaved Channel Grouping（ICG）策略以跨步方式划分潜在通道，将信息分散到各个数据包，并控制包大小；3) 采用两层双分支自回归结构缩短依赖链，限制丢包引起的级联误差传播。

Result: 在20%丢包率下，相比LossResilientLIC平均PSNR提升1.84 dB，PSNR方差降低一个数量级。仅在均匀随机丢包下训练的模型，能够泛化到Gilbert-Elliott信道建模的突发丢包场景，且优于专门针对该条件训练的现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过在打包阶段和熵编码阶段同时进行针对性的结构设计，可以显著提升学习式图像压缩在丢包环境下的鲁棒性，同时保持良好的重建质量和稳定性。

Abstract: Learned image compression (LIC) has achieved impressive rate-distortion performance. However, existing methods remain highly vulnerable to packet loss, a common challenge in satellite and emergency communications. This vulnerability stems from non-uniform information distribution at the packetization stage and sequential decoding dependencies at the entropy coding stage. We propose an end-to-end loss-resilient image compression scheme that addresses both. Before packetization, we introduce an Inter-Channel Redistribution (ICR) mechanism to redistribute channel energy, preventing critical information concentrating in a small subset of channels. Then, an Interleaved Channel Grouping (ICG) strategy partitions latent channels in a strided manner to disperse information across packets, with each packet kept within constrained sizes. To limit cascading errors from lost packets, we adopt a two-layer dual-branch autoregressive structure to shorten the dependency chain. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms existing approaches in both reconstruction quality and stability. At 20% packet loss, it achieves an average PSNR gain of 1.84 dB over LossResilientLIC while reducing PSNR variance by an order of magnitude. Notably, trained under uniform random loss only, our model generalizes to bursty loss modeled by the Gilbert-Elliott channel, outperforming methods explicitly trained for such conditions.

</details>


### [108] [AlbumentationsX: One Augmentation Pipeline for Images and Related Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11123)
*Vladimir Iglovikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AlbumentationsX是一个数据增强库，通过将所有变换参数（变换列表、概率、标注设置、随机种子）封装在一个Compose对象中，确保每次调用时随机值一次性生成并应用到训练样本的所有部分（图像、掩码、框、关键点等），避免因图像与标注分别应用不同随机变换而导致的错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据增强过程中，图像和其对应的标注（掩码、边界框、关键点等）经常接收到不同的随机变换（如裁剪坐标不一致），导致标注与图像错位，从而破坏训练样本的质量。这种问题源于代码路径分别选择随机值。

Method: AlbumentationsX将所有变换列表、概率、标注设置和随机种子统一封装在一个Compose对象中。每次调用时只选择一次随机值，并将其应用到训练样本的所有受支持部分。库将每个对象的掩码、框和标签绑定在一起，支持自定义变换，并能保存管道定义、展示单次调用过程及重放。

Result: 通过统一随机值生成和集中管理变换配置，有效避免了数据错位问题。该库支持在文件解码为数组之后、PyTorch批处理之前执行变换，同时保留实践者对变换操作是否保持标签有效性的判断权。

Conclusion: AlbumentationsX通过集中式Compose设计解决了数据增强中标注错位的核心问题，提供了更可靠、可复现且高度可定制的数据增强流程。

Abstract: Augmentation can corrupt a training example when an image and its annotations receive different random changes. A crop must use the same coordinates for the image, mask, boxes, keypoints, stereo views, video frames, or volume. Code paths that choose these values separately can silently misalign the data.
  AlbumentationsX keeps the transform list, probabilities, annotation settings, and random seed in one Compose object. Each call chooses random values once and applies them to every supported part of the training example. The library keeps each object's mask, box, and label together and lets projects add their own transforms. It can also save the pipeline definition, show what happened in one call, and run that call again.
  The examples place Compose after files have been decoded into arrays and before PyTorch groups examples into a batch. AlbumentationsX executes the declared transforms. Practitioners still decide whether a flip, crop, color change, or other operation preserves the correct label for their task.

</details>


### [109] [Is There Really a Camouflaged Object? Towards Realistic Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11135)
*Huafeng Chen,Yueming Lyu,Chenyang Si,Wende Tan,Liucheng Guo,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OPC16K基准数据集和OPCNet网络，解决开放环境下伪装目标检测（COD）的假阳性问题。OPC16K包含16,245张来自14个来源的图像，分为伪装目标、纯背景和非伪装目标三类；OPCNet将COD从纯分割任务重构为定位与伪装存在性推理的联合问题，通过分层存在性推理、相似度感知伪装关系建模和存在性感知特征精炼，在保持分割精度的同时显著减少负样本上的假阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有COD方法基于封闭世界假设开发，默认每张输入图像都包含伪装目标，忽视了现实场景中可能存在纯背景或非伪装目标的情况，导致模型在开放环境下产生严重假阳性。为此需要构建更贴近实际场景的基准和相应算法。

Method: 提出了两个核心贡献：(1) OPC16K基准数据集，含三类图像（伪装目标、纯背景、非伪装目标），支持分割质量与负样本拒绝能力的综合评估；(2) OPCNet网络，包含分层存在性推理区分三种场景、相似度感知伪装关系建模捕捉前景-背景伪装线索、存在性感知特征精炼利用存在性预测调节分割特征。

Result: 在OPC16K基准上，OPCNet在提出的现实COD评估协议下取得优越性能，显著降低负样本上的假阳性，同时保持准确的伪装目标分割精度。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入真实场景基准和存在性感知网络，将COD从封闭世界假设推进到开放世界场景，有效解决了负样本假阳性问题，为后续研究提供了新的评估标准和方法范式。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) aims to segment objects that are visually concealed in their surroundings and has attracted increasing attention in recent years. However, most existing COD methods are developed under a closed-world assumption, where each input image is assumed to contain a camouflaged object. This assumption ignores realistic scenarios with pure backgrounds or non-camouflaged objects, causing existing models to produce severe false positives when deployed in open-world environments. To address this limitation, we propose OPC16K, a large-scale benchmark for realistic COD. OPC16K contains 16,245 images from 14 sources and is carefully organized into camouflaged-object images, pure background images, and non-camouflaged-object images, enabling comprehensive evaluation of both segmentation quality and negative-sample rejection. Based on this benchmark, we further propose OPCNet, a presence-aware camouflage network that reformulates COD from a pure segmentation task into a joint problem of object localization and camouflage existence reasoning. Specifically, OPCNet introduces hierarchical existence reasoning to distinguish CO, BG, and NOCOD scenarios, similarity-aware camouflage relation modeling to capture foreground-background camouflage cues, and existence-aware feature refinement to regulate segmentation features with existence predictions. Extensive experiments on OPC16K demonstrate that OPCNet achieves superior performance under the proposed realistic COD evaluation protocol, significantly reducing false positives on negative samples while maintaining accurate camouflaged-object segmentation. Code and dataset will be released at https://github.com/2231122/OPCOD.

</details>


### [110] [SAR2Agri: Learning SAR Intensity Representations for Agricultural Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11142)
*Moti Rattan Gupta,Anupam Sobti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个仅使用SAR强度影像的自监督学习管道，专用于农业监测。通过掩码和课程学习改进时间 pretext 任务，在SICKLE基准上作物类型映射IoU达84.9%，超越光学基线15.3个百分点和现有SAR基线2.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业监测面临复杂的时间、物候和气候动态挑战，SAR卫星具备全天候昼夜成像能力是关键工具。现有多模态遥感基础模型依赖光学影像配准，SAR专用模型主要聚焦目标检测、洪水制图和土地覆盖分类，缺乏专为农业应用设计、仅使用SAR强度影像的自监督预训练方法。近期基于光学影像的物候时间pretext任务表现优异，但尚未推广到SAR领域。

Method: 提出首个仅使用SAR强度影像的自监督学习管道：(1) 将物候启发的时间pretext任务扩展到SAR强度影像；(2) 通过掩码（masking）策略增强pretext任务；(3) 引入课程学习（curriculum learning）分阶段训练以提升预训练管道捕捉SAR物候特征的能力。

Result: 在SICKLE基准上，最终模型在作物类型映射任务上达到84.9% IoU，超越光学基线15.3个百分点，并超越现有SAR基线2.2个百分点，验证了所提预训练管道在SAR强度编码器训练中的有效性。

Conclusion: 仅使用SAR强度影像的自监督预训练管道能够高效捕获农业物候特征，在作物类型映射任务上优于依赖光学影像的多模态方法，为农业监测提供了不依赖光学数据可用性的新路径。

Abstract: Agricultural monitoring faces unique challenges, arising from the landscape's complex temporal, phenological, and climate dynamics, yet monitoring them is critical for ensuring food security. Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) satellites offer all-weather day-night imaging capability supporting key monitoring tasks including crop type mapping, yield prediction and phenological event detection. Existing multimodal remote sensing foundation models including TerraMind and CopernicusFM learn SAR representations by grounding them in optical imagery using joint encoding and contrastive learning techniques, while SAR-specific foundation models such as SAR-JEPA, SARMAE, and SAR-W-MixMAE primarily focus on target detection, flood mapping, and land cover classification applications. Recent work has introduced phenology inspired temporal pretext tasks with optical imagery which has shown strong performance on agricultural downstream tasks. In this work, we propose the first self-supervised learning pipeline focused on using only SAR intensity imagery for agricultural applications. We improve the temporal pretext tasks through masking and curriculum learning to enhance the pretraining pipeline's ability to capture phenological features from SAR. On the SICKLE benchmark, our final model achieves 84.9% IoU on crop type mapping, outperforming optical baselines (by 15.3 pt) and existing SAR baselines (by 2.2 pt), demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed pipeline for pretraining SAR intensity encoders for agricultural monitoring.

</details>


### [111] [PRMU: A Corpus-Free Benchmark for Person-Centric Knowledge Unlearning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11149)
*Huafeng Chen,Yueming Lyu,Ziyuan Chen,Wenda Tan,Chenyang Si,Liucheng Guo,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PRMU基准，用于评估无原始语料的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）人物知识删除场景，并引入SGPE轻量级消融基线，在遗忘效果、局部保留和通用能力之间实现竞争性平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM机器遗忘方法通常假设可以访问原始的遗忘和保留语料，但在实际删除场景中这些语料往往不可获得，导致现有方法难以适用。

Method: 构建PRMU基准，聚焦自然获得的人物相关知识，通过多样化的文本和视觉探针（包括对抗性评估和细粒度局部性分析）评估目标知识移除与相关知识的保留。同时提出SGPE基线方法，包含知识位移、受保护的参数空间编辑和局部感知的多模态控制。

Result: 实验表明现有遗忘方法常面临不理想的遗忘-局部性权衡，在激进的遗忘设置下局部性显著退化，且易受多模态知识重激活影响；SGPE在目标遗忘、局部保留和通用多模态效用之间提供了有竞争力的权衡。

Conclusion: PRMU可推动现实且可扩展的多模态机器遗忘研究，代码和数据集已公开。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in storing and recalling rich person-related knowledge, raising increasing concerns about reliable knowledge removal. However, existing machine unlearning approaches for MLLMs typically assume access to original forget and retain corpora, which are often unavailable in realistic deletion scenarios. To address this limitation, we introduce PRMU, a benchmark for evaluating corpus-free multimodal unlearning under realistic person-centric deletion requests. PRMU focuses on naturally acquired person-related knowledge and evaluates whether models can remove target knowledge while preserving related knowledge through diverse textual and visual probes, including adversarial evaluation and fine-grained locality analysis. To facilitate research in this setting, we further introduce Similarity-Gated Projection Editing (SGPE), a lightweight corpus-free unlearning baseline with knowledge displacement, protected parameter-space editing, and locality-aware multimodal control. Extensive experiments on representative MLLMs reveal that existing unlearning methods often suffer from unfavorable forgetting-locality trade-offs, with significant locality degradation under aggressive forgetting settings, and remain vulnerable to multimodal knowledge reactivation. Meanwhile, SGPE provides a competitive trade-off between target forgetting, locality preservation, and general multimodal utility. We hope PRMU can facilitate future research toward realistic and scalable multimodal machine unlearning. Code and dataset will be released at https://github.com/2231122/PRMU.

</details>


### [112] [CausalSplat: Towards Comprehensive Hierarchical Reasoning in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11150)
*Jiayu Ding,Meilu Song,Yun Chen,Wei Gao,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出因果3D高斯分割任务，构建Causal-LERF和Causal-ScanNet基准，并设计CausalSplat框架，将视觉语言模型与3D场景图结合，以解决现有3DGS方法无法处理隐式意图、复杂空间约束和常识推理的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3D高斯泼溅的开放词汇场景理解方法局限于显式查询，无法处理实际具身交互所需的隐式意图、复杂空间约束和常识推理，存在明显能力缺口。

Method: 提出CausalSplat框架，将视觉语言模型与3D场景图深度融合，将显式结构感知与隐式逻辑推理进行解耦，实现对因果和常识推理的3D高斯分割。

Result: CausalSplat在提出的推理基准上达到最先进性能，并在标准指代分割和开放词汇3D分割任务上展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将3D场景图与视觉语言模型结合，可以有效弥合显式分割与隐式推理之间的鸿沟，为具身智能场景理解提供新的解决思路。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has advanced open vocabulary scene understanding, existing methods remain confined to explicit queries. They struggle to interpret implicit intents, complex spatial constraints, and commonsense reasoning required for practical embodied interactions. To address this gap, we introduce the task of reasoning 3D Gaussian segmentation and construct two benchmarks, Causal-LERF and Causal-ScanNet. These benchmarks systematically evaluate commonsense, spatial, affordance, and counterfactual reasoning. Evaluations reveal that current state of the art methods perform poorly on these reasoning challenges. Therefore, we propose CausalSplat, a framework that integrates vision-language models with 3D scene graphs to disentangle explicit structural perception from implicit logical inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CausalSplat achieves state of the art performance on our reasoning benchmarks while showing strong generalizability on standard referring and open vocabulary 3D segmentation tasks. Project Page: https://jiayuding031020.github.io/CausalSplat

</details>


### [113] [MultiModal Code-Switching: Interleaving Visual Objects into Language for Explicit Object-Level Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11167)
*Changhao Xiang,Shangyu Xing,Zhen Wu,Jianbing Zhang,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多模态代码切换（MMCS）预训练范式，通过将文本实体替换为对应的视觉对象，提供显式的对象级监督，解决MLLM中图像级对齐存在的指称模糊问题，仅用50K样本即可匹配或超越基于600K图文对训练的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs主要依赖图文对进行图像级对齐，将全局图像表示映射到长文本描述，但这种对齐存在指称模糊问题：模型难以从全局表示中推断多个视觉对象与文本实体之间的对应关系，导致数据效率低下且语义接地能力不佳。

Method: 提出MultiModal Code-Switching (MMCS)预训练范式，受语言学中代码切换现象启发，在训练数据中交替插入视觉与语言，将文本实体替换为对应的视觉对象，强制执行局部视觉-语言接地。同时开发可扩展的数据合成流水线，生成包含77.3万样本且具有精确对象-实体对应关系的数据集。

Result: MMCS具有高数据效率：仅用5万样本即可匹配或超越在60万图文对上训练的模型。此外，MMCS在不同模型规模上持续提升视觉接地和感知能力。

Conclusion: 对象级对齐预训练范式MMCS有效解决了图像级对齐的指称模糊问题，显著提高数据效率，并稳定提升MLLMs的视觉接地与感知能力。

Abstract: Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) predominantly rely on image-text pairs for modality alignment pretraining, mapping global image representations to long textual descriptions. However, this image-level alignment suffers from referential ambiguity: models struggle to infer the correspondences between multiple visual objects and textual entities from the global representation, leading to data inefficiency and suboptimal semantic grounding. To address this, we propose MultiModal Code-Switching (MMCS), a novel pretraining paradigm that provides explicit object-level supervision. Inspired by the linguistic phenomenon of code-switching, MMCS interleaves vision and language by replacing textual entities with their corresponding visual objects, enforcing local vision-language grounding. We further develop a scalable data synthesis pipeline to generate a pretraining dataset of 773K samples with accurate object-entity correspondences. Experiments show that MMCS is highly data-efficient: with only 50K samples, it matches or surpasses models trained on 600K image-text pairs. Furthermore, MMCS consistently improves visual grounding and perception capabilities across varying model scales.

</details>


### [114] [Test-Time Self-Evolving GUI Visual Grounding via Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11191)
*Shiyu Xuan,Zechao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个测试时自进化框架（Test-Time Self-Evolving），通过探索-评估-反思-内化的闭环流程，使GUI视觉定位模型在部署后无需人工标注即可持续适应未见界面，在六个基准上平均提升7.4%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI视觉定位模型部署后参数冻结，无法适应未见过的新界面；虽然近期有方法尝试通过测试时强化学习进行适配，但无法对失败的探索进行反思，缺乏自我纠错能力。

Method: 构建探索（Exploration）、评估（Evaluation）、反思（Reflection）、内化（Internalization）闭环。引入基于MLLM的反思器评估生成结果并提供推理反馈；提出反思引导的on-policy自蒸馏方法，将高层推理转化为密集token级监督；设计对比校准方法防止错误的自回归前缀污染失败探索中的监督信号。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上验证了框架有效性，平均准确率相比基础模型提升7.4%。

Conclusion: 这是首个成功利用on-policy自蒸馏实现GUI视觉定位测试时自适应的工作，填补了部署后适配的空白，完善了GUI代理的自进化能力。

Abstract: GUI Visual Grounding is a fundamental capability for GUI agents. Existing models typically freeze their parameters after deployment, limiting their ability to adapt to unseen interfaces. Although recent methods attempt to adapt models via test-time reinforcement learning, they cannot reflect upon failed exploration. To overcome this, we propose a Test-Time Self-Evolving framework that enables models to improve after deployment without human-annotated ground truth. It constructs a closed-loop of Exploration, Evaluation, Reflection, and Internalization. Specifically, the agent first explores unseen interfaces by predicting grounding coordinates for given instructions. To evaluate these explorations, we introduce an MLLM-based Reflector to assess the generated results and provide the corresponding reasoning reflections. To internalize reflection knowledge into the model weights, we propose Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation, which translates high-level reasoning into dense token-level supervision via a conditioned self-teacher. Furthermore, we design a Contrastive Calibration method to prevent incorrect auto-regressive prefixes from corrupting the supervisory signals during failed explorations. Extensive experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate our framework's effectiveness, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 7.4% over the base model. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to successfully exploit on-policy self-distillation for test-time adaptation in GUI visual grounding. By filling the gap in post-deployment adaptation, our framework completes the self-evolving capability of GUI agents. The code will be released.

</details>


### [115] [VidForensics-M1: Meta-Detection Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Temporal Grounding for AI-Generated Video Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11201)
*Bowei Liu,Zheng Lu,Yuhan Bian,Xinchen Zhang,Xingming Shui,Yuesheng Huang,Xuhuan Li,Zihao Liu,Yifan Yang,Jun Zhou,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将元检测（meta-detection）引入AI生成视频检测领域，提出VidForensics-M1框架，通过证据引导的奖励重分配机制，在强化学习中联合优化预测标签与支持性证据，利用时间定位（temporal grounding）作为客观可验证的证据信号，实现鲁棒且可泛化的AI生成视频伪造检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的视频检测器主要依赖监督微调或标签级强化学习，粗粒度监督限制了模型对未见场景和新出现视频生成器的泛化能力。文本推理虽然提供伪造痕迹的语义描述，但其生成和验证依赖外部模型，易受幻觉和语义偏差影响。因此需要更客观、可验证的证据信号来提升检测可靠性。

Method: 1) 提出一种自动化数据构建管道，通过边界帧条件视频生成模型替换时间片段，生成配对的真实-伪造视频；2) 引入证据引导奖励重分配（Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution）机制，根据证据质量在标签正确的响应之间重新分配奖励，实现证据感知的信用分配；3) 利用时间定位作为客观可验证的证据信号，在强化学习框架中联合优化预测标签和证据生成。

Result: 大量实验表明，VidForensics-M1能够有效利用可验证的时间证据，实现鲁棒且可泛化的AI生成视频检测，显著优于现有方法在未见场景和新生成器上的表现。

Conclusion: 时间定位证据比文本推理更客观可验证，将其纳入标签级强化学习优化能够保持可靠的标签监督，同时鼓励检测器获得细粒度、可验证的伪造定位能力，为AI生成视频检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation models have significantly improved the realism of synthetic videos, blurring the boundary between generated and authentic content and raising concerns about misinformation. Existing MLLM-based detectors mainly rely on supervised fine-tuning or label-level reinforcement learning, where coarse supervision limits generalization to unseen scenarios and emerging video generators. To overcome these limitations, we are the first to introduce \textbf{meta-detection} into AI-generated video detection, enabling reliable forgery detection by jointly optimizing predicted labels and supporting evidence within reinforcement learning. This paradigm requires reliable evidence signals and effective mechanisms to integrate them into label-level optimization. Textual rationales provide semantic descriptions of forgery artifacts, but their generation and verification depend on external models, making supervision vulnerable to hallucinations and semantic biases. In contrast, temporal grounding provides more objective and verifiable evidence, as manipulated intervals can be precisely controlled during forgery construction. Based on this insight, we propose an automated data construction pipeline that generates paired real-fake videos by replacing temporal segments with boundary-frame-conditioned video generation models. Furthermore, we introduce \textbf{Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution}, which performs evidence-aware credit assignment by redistributing rewards among label-correct responses according to evidence quality. This preserves reliable label supervision while encouraging detectors to acquire fine-grained and verifiable forgery localization capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textbf{VidForensics-M1} effectively leverages verifiable temporal evidence to achieve robust and generalizable AI-generated video detection.

</details>


### [116] [Capturing Uncertainty in Human Motion for Representation Learning in Soccer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11203)
*Yizhou Xu,Lars Bretzner,Tiesheng Wang,Atsuto Maki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于未来运动预测的自监督表示学习框架，用于理解足球场景中的3D骨架人体运动。通过条件模块建模离散化未来运动的概率分布，显式学习多模态运动模式，在预测精度和下游任务泛化上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体运动本质具有不确定性，仅预测单一未来轨迹无法充分捕捉运动动态，因此需要建模多个合理的未来运动以学习更有效的表示。

Method: 提出一个条件预测模块，在3D欧几里得空间中对离散化未来运动建模概率分布，并利用未来轨迹提供显式监督来学习多模态运动的表示。

Result: 在大规模足球运动员追踪数据上显著提升了运动预测精度；学习到的表示可有效迁移到多个足球下游应用任务，展现出强大的跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过未来运动预测的自监督学习，结合多模态概率建模，能够学习到具有强泛化能力的骨骼运动表示，适用于多种下游应用。

Abstract: This paper presents a self-supervised representation learning framework for understanding 3D skeleton-based human motion in soccer, using future motion prediction as the learning objective. Since human motion is inherently uncertain, accounting for multiple plausible futures is essential for capturing the underlying motion dynamics and learning effective representations. To this end, we introduce a conditioning module for motion prediction that models a probabilistic distribution over discretized future motions in 3D Euclidean space, learning multimodality with explicit supervision from future trajectories. Experiments on large-scale soccer player tracking data show that our approach substantially improves motion prediction accuracy. Moreover, the learned representations effectively transfer to multiple soccer downstream applications, demonstrating strong cross-task generalization.

</details>


### [117] [AdvFD: Boosting Visual Generation via Adversarial Fr'echet Distance Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11205)
*Mingju Gao,Jingkai Zhou,Kun Gai,Changqian Yu,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性Fréchet距离（AdvFD），通过引入校准的对抗学习表示来补充静态预训练特征空间的不足，解决Fréchet目标优化中出现的"Fréchet hacking"（指标提升但视觉质量下降）问题，并在一步生成器后训练中取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fréchet距离作为分布级目标在生成器后训练中表现优异，但直接优化会引发"Fréchet hacking"——目标指标持续提升，而视觉质量和在其他特征空间中的Fréchet对齐却停滞或恶化。作者将失败原因归因于现有Fréchet损失所使用的静态预训练特征空间，这些特征空间对真实与生成分布之间的差异提供了不完整且固定的视角。

Method: AdvFD在原有FD-Loss的静态表示目标基础上，补充一个经校准的对抗学习表示。该可学习表示通过对抗方式最大化真实与生成样本之间的Fréchet差异，而生成器则在最终的自适应特征空间中最小化相同差异。为防止对抗表示通过特征放大平凡地增大目标，作者引入真实特征白化（real-feature whitening），用于归一化特征的尺度和协方差几何，从而稳定min-max优化过程。

Result: 大量实验表明，AdvFD在JiT和pMF两种骨干网络上以及不同模型规模下，均能一致地改进一步生成器的后训练效果。

Conclusion: 通过对抗学习自适应特征空间并配合真实特征白化，AdvFD有效克服了静态特征空间的局限性，缓解了Fréchet hacking问题，为生成器后训练提供了更鲁棒的分布级优化目标。

Abstract: Fréchet distance has recently emerged as an effective distribution-level objective for generator post-training, complementing the conventional sample-level diffusion and flow-matching losses. However, directly optimizing Fréchet objectives can cause Fréchet hacking. The target metrics keep improving, but visual quality and Fréchet alignment in other feature spaces may stagnate or deteriorate. We attribute this failure to the static pretrained feature spaces used by existing Fréchet losses. These feature spaces provide incomplete and fixed views of the differences between real and generated distributions. To address this limitation, we propose Adversarial Fréchet Distance (AdvFD), which complements the static representation targets in FD-Loss with a calibrated adversarially learned representation. AdvFD augments the original static Fréchet objective with a learnable representation that adversarially maximizes the Fréchet discrepancy between real and generated samples, while the generator minimizes the same discrepancy in the resulting adaptive feature space. To prevent the adversarial representation from trivially increasing the objective through feature amplification, we further introduce real-feature whitening, which normalizes its scale and covariance geometry and stabilizes the min--max optimization. Extensive experiments show that AdvFD consistently improves one-step generator post-training across both JiT and pMF backbones and across different model scales.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Protection Levels for Vision-Based Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10023)
*Olivia Beyer Bruvik,Romeo Valentin,Marc R. Schlichting,Don Walker,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在基于视觉的跑道位姿估计框架中扩展了保护级别（protection levels）的推导，为非线性PnP问题提供了在未检测到故障情况下仍有效的位姿误差概率边界，并分析了测量冗余、像素级预测不确定性和跑道距离的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉导航可用于补充卫星导航系统，但认证要求完整性保障，必须考虑故障测量带来的风险。现有工作虽提出了带故障检测的概率视觉位姿估计流水线，但缺乏保护级别的推导来提供严格的安全边界。

Method: 将RAIM（接收机自主完整性监测）的思路扩展到非线性透视n点（PnP）问题，提出了一种计算保护级别的算法，直接覆盖飞机位姿的全部六个自由度（位置和姿态），并在航空场景下进行验证。

Result: 算法能够为六自由度位姿提供保护级别，并量化了测量冗余度、像素级预测不确定性以及跑道距离对保护级别的影响，通过示例跑道展示了保护级别中的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于视觉的跑道导航提供了形式化的完整性保障框架，使保护级别在未检测到故障时依然成立，推动了视觉导航在航空认证场景中的可用性。

Abstract: Vision-based navigation complements Global Navigation Satellite Systems, but certification demands integrity guarantees that account for faulty measurements. Previous work presented a probabilistic computer vision pipeline for runway-based pose estimation with fault detection inspired by Receiver Autonomous Integrity Monitoring. This work extends that framework by deriving protection levels, which provide probabilistic bounds on pose error that remain valid under undetected faults. We present an algorithm for computing protection levels for the nonlinear Perspective-$n$-Point problem applied to an aviation setting. The algorithm covers all six degrees of freedom of the aircraft pose (position and orientation) directly. We analyze the effect of measurement redundancy, pixel-level prediction uncertainty, and runway distance on the resulting protection levels. To make the results tangible, we demonstrate tradeoffs in the protection levels on an illustrative runway example.

</details>


### [119] [The Impact of Operational-Data Fidelity when Assessing Safety-Critical Autonomous-Vehicle Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10025)
*Kizito Salako,Rabiu Tsoho Muhammad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展保守贝叶斯推断（CBI）技术，提出了一种检验基于不充分详细运营数据所得可靠性声明稳健性的统计方法，并在自动驾驶车辆安全评估场景中展示了数据粒度不足如何导致可靠性声明过度乐观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键软件的运营历史数据（如成功/失败事件序列）虽能为可靠性声明提供统计支持，但这些数据可能缺乏对过去失败事件足够详细的描述，导致基于这些数据的可靠性评估无法充分考虑失败的重要特征。

Method: 扩展保守贝叶斯推断（CBI）技术，将其应用于可靠性评估中，用于检验由不充分详细的运营数据推导出的可靠性声明的稳健性。

Result: 在自动驾驶车辆安全评估场景中证明，即使评估者努力保守地使用数据，由不充分细粒度数据推导出的可靠性声明仍可能危险地过度乐观。首次给出了数据保真度对评估影响的保守估计。

Conclusion: 低保真度数据即使被保守使用也可能是天真的，这一发现澄清了先前关于贝叶斯软件可靠性评估中统计模型保真度影响的工作，并强调了运营数据细节充分性对可靠性评估的重要性。

Abstract: For safety-critical software, data from the software's operational past (e.g. a sequence of success and failure events experienced by the software) can provide strong statistical support for reliability claims about the software. However, such data might not describe past software failure events in sufficient detail, and this might leave a reliability assessment (based on this data) unable to account for important features of past software failures. In this paper, by extending conservative Bayesian inference (CBI) techniques used in reliability assessment, we illustrate a principled statistical approach for checking the robustness of reliability claims derived from insufficiently detailed operational data. We demonstrate the extent to which insufficient detail in operational data can undermine software reliability claims in autonomous vehicle (AV) safety assessment scenarios. Reliability claims derived from insufficiently fine-grained data might be dangerously optimistic, despite a concerted effort by an assessor to use such data conservatively during the assessment. While these findings are consistent with previous work on the impact of statistical model fidelity in Bayesian software reliability assessments, our work clarifies why attempts to use low-fidelity data conservatively can be naive, and we give the first conservative estimates of the impact of data fidelity on assessments.

</details>


### [120] [Navigating the Proximity-Safety Balance: Constraint Decomposition for Human Following in Pedestrian Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10056)
*Shiting Gong,Jianpeng Yao,Jinfeng Wang,Marco Pavone,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多约束强化学习方法，将人群中的目标跟随任务分解为稀疏任务奖励和独立成本约束，通过可调成本阈值实现显式的邻近-安全平衡，并结合人体运动预测不确定性来增强安全性能。实验和真实机器人部署验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法将跟随目标的两个竞争目标（保持靠近与安全导航）编码为单一稠密奖励，导致邻近-安全平衡隐式且难以跨场景调节，尤其在密集和不确定人群环境中效果不佳。

Method: 将目标跟随任务建模为多约束RL问题：一个稀疏任务奖励加多个独立成本约束，每个约束通过具有直接行为含义的成本阈值管理，而非隐式权重比值；同时量化人体运动预测不确定性并集成到RL成本中。

Result: 在分布内和分布外场景的实验中，该方法相比基线实现了更有效的邻近-安全平衡；真实机器人部署进一步验证了方法在现实场景中的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过多约束RL框架和不确定性感知成本设计，该方法能够显式且可调地控制跟随任务中的邻近-安全权衡，在密集人群中具备良好的泛化性和实际部署能力。

Abstract: Following a target human in crowded environments involves an inherent conflict between staying close to the target and navigating safely among surrounding pedestrians and obstacles. This conflict becomes more severe in dense scenarios, where aggressive following risks collisions and conservative margins lead to target loss, especially when pedestrian behaviors are unfamiliar or unpredictable. Existing reinforcement learning (RL) methods typically encode these competing objectives into a single dense reward, but the resulting proximity-safety balance is implicit and difficult to adjust across conditions. To address this, we decompose the human-following task into a sparse task reward and independent cost constraints within a multi-constraint RL formulation, where each constraint is managed through cost thresholds with direct behavioral meaning rather than implicit reward weight ratios, allowing explicit and tunable control over the trade-off. We further quantify the prediction uncertainty of human motions and integrate these estimates into the RL costs to enhance safety under unpredictable conditions. Extensive experiments across both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings demonstrate that our method achieves an effective proximity-safety balance compared to baselines. Real-robot deployment further validates the feasibility of our method in real-world scenarios. More details are available on our project page: https://nav-ps-balance.github.io/.

</details>


### [121] [Whole-Body Planning for Humanoids Navigating Confined Spaces via Self-Collision Avoidance References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10220)
*Carlos Gonzalez,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个三阶段全身规划框架，在运动学可达的刚体体积上直接进行路径规划，通过可微碰撞避免和可达性约束生成体积引导，驱动全阶轨迹优化器，并训练残差强化学习策略实现鲁棒在线执行，在Unitree G1人形机器人上验证了极端受限环境（约束比Cr<1.5）下的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轨迹优化器在高度受限环境中（如密集障碍物和自碰撞约束）表现不佳，基于粒子抽象的样条难以处理大规模碰撞空间，容易陷入较差的局部最优解。需要一种能够直接在刚体体积层面进行规划并产出高质量参考轨迹的方法。

Method: 提出三阶段全身规划框架：(1)直接在运动学可达的刚体体积上制定运动学路径规划；(2)将可微碰撞避免集成到可达性约束的公式中，合成体积引导用于指导全阶轨迹优化器；(3)将优化后的轨迹作为高质量参考，训练残差强化学习策略以支持鲁棒的在线执行。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上，跨越三个基准测试平台，超越NIST应急响应标准，实现受限约束比（Cr<1.5）。该框架能生成12至18秒复杂任务下涉及脚部和手部接触的可行轨迹，而标准基线方法失败；学习到的策略在大量域随机化的物理仿真中成功跟踪这些计划。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于体积的运动学规划与全阶轨迹优化相结合的框架，有效解决了人形机器人在高度受限环境下的全身运动规划难题，生成的轨迹可作为强化学习策略的高质量参考，实现鲁棒的在线执行，有望满足实际应急响应等应用需求。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion in highly confined environments requires navigating dense environmental obstacles and complex self-collision bounds while maintaining multi-contact dynamic feasibility. Traditional trajectory optimizers frequently struggle in these restricted spaces, as navigating the large collision space with splines on particle abstractions is insufficient and leads to poor local minima. To address this, we propose a three-stage whole-body planning framework that formulates kinematic path planning directly over kinematically reachable rigid-body volumes. By integrating differentiable collision avoidance into a reachability-constrained formulation, our framework synthesizes volume-informed guides that reliably guide a full-order trajectory optimizer over long horizons. We show that these optimized plans serve as high-quality references to train a residual reinforcement learning policy for robust online execution. We validate our approach on the Unitree G1 humanoid across three benchmark testbeds exceeding NIST emergency response standards, achieving restricted confinement ratios ($C_r < 1.5$). Our framework generates feasible trajectories across 12-to-18-second tasks with complex foot and hand contacts where standard baselines fail, while the learned policy successfully tracks these plans under extensive domain randomization in physics simulation.

</details>


### [122] [FACT: Failure-Aware Causal Training for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10232)
*Quanquan Peng,Yutong Liang,Rui Yan,Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FACT是一种因果世界-动作模型（WAM），通过将未来视频和任务进度预测与已执行动作关联起来，利用失败轨迹数据来监督动作后果，从而减少成功偏差的未来幻觉，并在仿真和真实双臂操作任务中优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）大多仅在成功示范数据上训练，缺乏预测坏动作后果的能力，导致模型对未来状态产生成功偏置的幻觉，难以在失败场景下做出正确决策。

Method: 提出FACT因果世界-动作模型，其核心是动作条件化的接口设计：模型预测未来视频和任务进度时都依赖于已执行的动作条件。这种设计使失败回合数据也能用于监督动作后果，将坏动作转化为有效的未来目标而非被丢弃。同时会使用失败感知训练使进度预测器同时对成功和失败的动作结果保持敏感，推理阶段可选地对候选动作进行评分。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实双臂操作任务上的大量实验表明，FACT优于多种现有基线方法；随着失败数据逐步纳入训练，模型性能持续提升；且在坏动作条件下能有效减少成功偏置的未来幻觉。

Conclusion: 通过将失败数据引入世界模型的训练并建立动作-未来预测之间的因果联系，可以显著提升世界-动作模型在真实操作任务上的泛化能力和鲁棒性，为利用失败经验改进物理先验提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Recent world-action models (WAMs) show that co-training policies with future prediction can provide physical priors for action generation. Building on the future-prediction ability of video models, many WAMs generate future videos and recover actions with inverse-dynamics models, or use these predicted videos as goal conditions for action generation. In both cases, the world model is trained mostly on successful demonstrations and has little reason to predict the consequences of bad actions. We introduce FACT, a causal World-Action Model that predicts future video and task progress conditioned on the executed action. This action-conditioned interface allows failure rollouts to supervise action consequences, turning bad actions into valid future targets rather than being discarded. Failure-aware training makes the progress predictor aware of both successful and failed action outcomes, which can optionally be used to score sampled action candidates at inference. Extensive experiments on simulation and real-world bimanual manipulation tasks show that FACT outperforms many existing baselines, improves as failure data are incorporated into training, and reduces success-biased future hallucination under bad actions. See more details at https://fact-wam.github.io/

</details>


### [123] [A Neural Network Based Teleoperation for Remote Controlled Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10367)
*Ning Ding,Azim Eskandarian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于波变量（WV）与自适应径向基函数网络（RBFN）相结合的单边遥操作车辆框架，在随机通信延迟下保证无源性稳定，并通过无模型自适应补偿车辆动力学不确定性。仿真对比PID、LQR、MPC和NMPC，RBFN在抗扰动鲁棒性和计算效率上均占优；1/10比例实车配合4G网络的硬件在环实验验证了其实际可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接遥操作车辆面临两大瓶颈：一是通信延迟严重影响系统稳定性；二是操作员无法感知未建模的环境扰动（如空气阻力、路面坡度）以及高度非线性的轮胎-路面动力学，导致轨迹跟踪精度和安全性受限。

Method: 提出单边遥操作框架，核心由两部分构成：(1) 波变量（WV）方法在随机延迟下被动保证系统稳定性；(2) 自适应RBFN在线补偿车辆特定不确定性。与现有面向双边机械臂的WV-神经网络架构不同，本框架针对车辆纵向和横向动力学设计了解耦的自适应律，并采用无模型RBFN避免预测控制的重计算负担。

Result: 仿真基准对比表明：RBFN在抵抗未建模扰动方面优于PID、LQR、MPC和NMPC，且执行时间比MPC和NMPC低数个数量级，适合资源受限的车辆边缘计算场景。基于4G网络的1/10比例车辆硬件在环实验验证了系统的实际可行性、安全性和在真实道路不确定性下的鲁棒轨迹跟踪能力。

Conclusion: 该单边遥操作框架通过WV+RBFN的组合，在理论稳定性和实际鲁棒性之间取得了良好平衡，为时延网络中车辆遥操作提供了一种计算高效且实用的解决方案，特别适用于边缘计算资源受限的应用场景。

Abstract: Direct teleoperation of vehicles faces critical technical bottlenecks: communication latency and the operator's inability to physically perceive unmodeled environmental disturbances (e.g., aerodynamic drag, bank angles) coupled with highly nonlinear tire-road dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose a tailored unilateral teleoperation framework. The system integrates the Wave Variable (WV) approach to passively guarantee stability under stochastic delays, and an adaptive Radial Basis Function Network (RBFN) to actively compensate for vehicle-specific uncertainties. Unlike existing WV-neural network architectures designed for bilateral robotic arms, our framework features decoupled adaptive laws specifically designed for vehicle longitudinal and lateral dynamics. Furthermore, compared to model-heavy predictive controllers, the model-free RBFN offers rapid online adaptation without heavy computational overhead. Building upon our preliminary theoretical formulation, this brief paper presents comprehensive comparative analyses and real-world hardware validations. Simulation benchmarks against PID, LQR, MPC, and NMPC demonstrate that the RBFN achieves superior robustness against unmodeled disturbances while requiring orders of magnitude less execution time than MPC and NMPC, making it ideal for resource-constrained vehicle edge computing. Finally, hardware-in-the-loop experiments using a 1/10th scale vehicle over a 4G network validate the system's practical feasibility, safety, and robust trajectory tracking under physical road uncertainties.

</details>


### [124] [Real-World Cooperative Bimanual Dexterous Grasp of Large Objects from Single-View Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10383)
*Ziming Li,Mingxuan Wu,Jiaqi Zhang,Hongfei Li,Yan Gan,Deqiang Ouyang,Ning Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个真实世界的双臂灵巧抓取框架，包含多模态数据集、基于DDPM的抓取配置生成模块，以及结合运动规划与在线抓取细化的执行策略。从单视角点云即可生成可执行的双臂抓取动作，无需完整3D物体模型，在双机械臂机器人上对未见物体取得了高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂灵巧抓取大物体是机器人操作中的关键挑战，但现有研究多集中于顺序操作而非协作抓取，且处理此类双臂任务的方法大多局限于仿真环境。其根本原因在于获取完整3D物体模型困难，以及生成物理上可行的抓取动作具有挑战性，亟需一个能在真实世界中稳定工作的双臂抓取框架。

Method: 提出一个真实世界双臂抓取框架，包含三个核心部分：(1) 多模态数据集，记录关节角度、视觉观测和力信号；(2) 基于去噪扩散概率模型(DDPM)的模块，从分割后的点云生成关节级抓取配置；(3) 执行策略，整合运动规划与在线抓取细化以确保物理稳定性和可行性。系统仅需单视角输入即可合成可执行的双臂抓取动作。

Result: 在双机械臂机器人上进行了实验，对具有不同几何形状和位姿的未见物体展现了高成功率；消融实验验证了系统各关键组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 该方法有效减少了系统对完整3D物体模型的依赖，实现了从单视角输入生成稳定、可执行的双臂抓取，并能在真实世界中取得良好的泛化性能，填补了双臂协作抓取在真实场景应用中的空白。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous grasping of large objects is a critical challenge in robotic manipulation. However, most existing studies focus on sequential manipulation rather than cooperative grasping, and methods addressing such bimanual tasks have largely been limited to simulation. These limitations stem from the difficulty of acquiring full 3D object models and generating physically plausible grasping actions. To fill this gap, we propose a real-world bimanual grasping framework that includes: a multimodal dataset capturing joint angles, visual observations and force signals; a Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM)-based module that generates joint-level grasp configurations from segmented point clouds; and an execution strategy that integrates motion planning with online grasp refinement to ensure physical stability and feasibility. Our approach enables the synthesis of executable bimanual grasps from single-view inputs, reducing dependence on complete 3D object models and ensuring stable real-world performance. Experiments on a dual-arm robot demonstrate high success rates across unseen objects with varying geometries and poses, and ablation studies confirm the contributions of key components of our system.

</details>


### [125] [Hip Energized Monopedal Hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10387)
*Shane Rozen-Levy,Griffon McMahon,Daniel Koditschek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的步态策略用于俯仰不受限的平面单足机器人：通过移动质心位置增强俯仰稳定力矩，将传统PD+前馈控制器产生的反作用力矩转变为克服阻尼能量损失的驱动力，从而实现稳定的周期性运动。混合平均化分析给出了步态的固定点和特征值闭式表达式，仿真和物理实验（速度1.02-1.77 m/s）验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面单足机器人在俯仰不受限情况下，传统PD+前馈控制器为稳定俯仰产生的反作用力矩通常被视为副作用或需要抑制，本文的动机在于利用这些反作用力矩来补充阻尼造成的能量损失，而非简单地将其视为负担，从而提高能量效率并实现稳定的步态。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 移动质心位置以增大俯仰稳定力矩，从而向步态注入能量；(2) 设计新的落脚策略，调整径向和角向自由度之间的能量分配，抵消耗散损失并实现用户指定的稳态速度与顶点高度平衡；(3) 采用混合平均化分析推导步态固定点和特征值的闭式解；(4) 在通用5连杆双足模型及Penn Jerboa精确模型上进行仿真验证。

Result: 仿真结果在两种模型上与解析预测有良好对应；Penn Jerboa物理实验实现了1.02 m/s至1.77 m/s（5.10至8.85腿长/秒）的稳定运动速度，且实际行为与分析预测高度吻合。

Conclusion: 这项工作证明了俯仰稳定产生的反作用力矩可以作为步态能量的重要来源，物理实验证实了理论分析的有效性，为能量利用更高效的平面单足机器人控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present a novel stepping strategy for pitch unlocked planar monopeds where the reaction torques from stabilizing pitch with a conventional PD + feedfoward controller are recruited to counteract energetic losses from damping. By moving the location of the mass center, our controller increases the pitch stabilization torque, thereby adding energy to the gait. A new stepping policy adjusts the distribution of energy between the radial and angular degrees of freedom to counteract dissipative losses and achieve a user specified balance between steady state fore-aft speed and apex height. Hybrid averaging analysis yields closed form expressions for the fixed points and eigenvalues of the resulting gait, lending insight into the interplay between the physical and control parameters' influence on performance. Simulation studies on a generic 5 link biped and a careful model of the Penn Jerboa reveal a useful correspondence to these analytical predictions. Physical experiments on the Penn Jerboa exhibit stable locomotion with speeds ranging from 1.02 m/s to 1.77 m/s (5.10 leg lengths/s to 8.85 leg lengths/s) in a manner effectively approximated by the mathematical analysis.

</details>


### [126] [PBD-AG: Persistent Baseline-Delta Active Graphs with Uncertainty-Aware Inspection for Long-Horizon Service Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10449)
*Shuo Bao,Wei Dong,Shuyue Zhang,Ming Shang,Yuchen Huang,Han Yu,Chengjie Xu,Yiheng Bi,Kai Sun,Fuchun Sun,Xinzhou Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PBD-AG，一种持久基线-动态活动图框架，用于长时程服务机器人在未知环境中的自主世界建模。它将机器人验证的稳定固定结构（基线）与可修订的动态物体事件（增量）解耦，通过自主探索引导基线、几何可见性门控缓解遮挡导致误删、图条件策略优化检查视点选择，从而构建可追溯、可验证的3D世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程服务机器人需要在未知环境中自主构建持久世界模型，并随任务相关物体的变化而更新。现有方法存在三大缺陷：在线建图累积定位与观测误差、静态场景表示无法捕捉物体持久变化、整体式视觉-语言预测缺乏可验证的3D几何证据。因此需要一个能自主建立并持续修订、且具有可验证几何证据的世界模型框架。

Method: PBD-AG框架的核心是"基线-增量"（baseline-delta）解耦思想：将机器人验证过的稳定结构物（基线）与可修订的动态物体事件（增量）分离。机器人通过机载探索自主引导结构基线，并检查所发现的固定物以建立分层的物体信念。框架维护跨几何、语义、身份、存在性和支撑关系的可靠性加权物体状态，利用几何可见性门控在遮挡条件下减少错误删除，并通过图条件策略选择检查视点，平衡目标覆盖率、移动代价、碰撞风险和冗余观测。

Result: 在多种环境及受控动态评估下的仿真实验表明，PBD-AG在聚合粗粒度固定物F1分数上优于能力匹配的对照组，并展现出更强的身份连续性和事件召回能力。此外，物理机器人定性演示验证了其与机载感知的集成，提供了可追溯的长时程机器人感知世界模型。

Conclusion: PBD-AG通过解耦稳定基线与动态事件、结合几何可见性门控和图条件视点规划，为长时程服务机器人提供了一种可验证、可追溯且能适应物体持续变化的持久世界建模方案，并在实验和物理演示中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon service robots require persistent world models that can be built autonomously in unseen environments and revised as task-relevant objects change. Existing methods rely on online mapping, which accumulates localization and observation errors, static scene representations that cannot capture persistent object changes, or holistic vision-language predictions that lack verifiable 3D geometric evidence. We present PBD-AG, a persistent baseline-delta active graph framework that decouples robot-verified stable fixtures from revisable dynamic object events. Under our framework, the robot autonomously bootstraps the structural baseline from onboard exploration and inspects discovered fixtures to ground hierarchical object beliefs. PBD-AG maintains reliability-weighted object states over geometry, semantics, identity, existence, and support relations, utilizing a geometric visibility gate to mitigate false deletions under occlusion. Inspection viewpoints are selected by a graph-conditioned policy that balances target coverage, travel cost, collision risk, and redundant observation. Simulation experiments in multiple environments and under controlled dynamic evaluation show higher aggregate coarse-fixture F1 than capability-matched controls, as well as stronger identity continuity and event recall. A qualitative physical-robot demonstration further illustrates integration with onboard sensing, providing a traceable world model for long-horizon robotic perception.The project page of PBD-AG is available at https://shuobao214.github.io/PBD-AG/

</details>


### [127] [Lost in Reconstruction: Aligning Action Representations with Language in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10484)
*Li Wenjie,Yash Jangir,Ignacy Stepka,Yash Agarwal,Marion Kipsang,Yonatan Bisk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SALT（语义对齐动作分词器），通过辅助损失让冻结的视觉语言模型从量化后的动作潜在表示中恢复指令文本，从而在动作表征中保留动词层面的语言语义信息，在SimplerEnv中达到71.9%的平均成功率，显著优于仅重建的VQ-VAE（42.7%）和FAST（31.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的动作表征通常仅针对原始动作空间中的L1/L2损失进行重建优化，数值上的接近并不反映语言学上有意义的区分。论文在BridgeV2上证明动作轨迹包含超越视觉状态变化的动词接地（verb-grounding）信息，而仅基于重建的离散分词会系统性侵蚀这些信息。

Method: SALT基于VQ-VAE风格分词器，引入一个辅助目标：要求冻结的视觉语言模型从量化后的动作潜在表示中恢复该回合的指令（instruction）。通过这种语义对齐约束，使得动作token在量化过程中保留与动词相关的语言信息，同时维持重建保真度。

Result: 在SimplerEnv中，SALT训练的policy平均成功率达到71.9%，显著高于仅重建的VQ-VAE分词器（42.7%）和FAST（31.2%）。SALT还发展出动词特化（verb-specialized）的编码码本，同时保持重建保真度。

Conclusion: 机器人动作轨迹本身就是语言接地的来源，在动作表征中保留这种结构可以大幅提升语言条件下的控制性能。SALT验证了将语义信息注入动作分词过程的有效性。

Abstract: Action verbs describe not only the physical outcomes of actions, but also how those actions are performed. Yet action representations in vision-language-action models (VLAs) are typically optimized for reconstruction under L1/L2 losses in raw action space, where numerical proximity need not reflect linguistically meaningful distinctions. On BridgeV2, we show that action trajectories contain verb-grounding information beyond visual state changes, and that reconstruction-only discrete tokenization systematically erodes this information. To address this problem, we introduce SALT, a Semantically ALigned action Tokenizer that augments a VQ-VAE-style tokenizer with an auxiliary objective requiring a frozen vision-language model to recover the episode instruction from quantized action latents. Policies trained with SALT achieve 71.9% average success in SimplerEnv, compared with 42.7% for a reconstruction-only VQ-VAE tokenizer and 31.2% for FAST. SALT also develops verb-specialized codes while maintaining reconstruction fidelity. These results show that robot action trajectories provide a source of language grounding and that preserving this structure in action representations can substantially improve language-conditioned control.

</details>


### [128] [JitTrack: Onboard Multi-Object Tracking Against Viewpoint Jitter for Agile UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10485)
*Yachun Shan,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出JitTrack，一个面向敏捷无人机机载多目标跟踪的主动框架，通过语义细化、运动感知查询校正和运动启发去噪训练策略，解决视角抖动导致的目标关联不准确问题，并构建了感知-规划-控制闭环跟踪管线，实现真实世界的无人机目标跟随。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷无人机在飞行过程中因姿态快速变化引起的相机自运动导致严重视角抖动，造成目标跨帧位移大、目标关联不准确、跟踪性能下降。现有无人机多目标跟踪方法主要在离线基准上评测，难以满足真实机载部署中对相机运动鲁棒性和主动目标跟随的实用需求。

Method: 基于查询的Transformer跟踪器构建JitTrack框架，引入三个核心模块：(1)语义细化提升新出现目标的检测能力；(2)运动感知查询校正补偿视角抖动导致的目标错位；(3)运动启发去噪训练策略模拟相机运动模式以提供鲁棒监督。此外开发了感知-规划-控制闭环跟踪管线，实现碰撞规避且物理可行的目标跟随。

Result: 在公开UAV多目标跟踪基准上的大量实验表明，相比基线方法取得了一致的性能提升；真实世界飞行实验验证了JitTrack在视角抖动下进行鲁棒机载视觉跟踪的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: JitTrack通过结合相机运动感知的设计和感知-规划-控制闭环管线，有效解决了敏捷无人机在视角抖动条件下的多目标跟踪难题，实现了从仿真基准到真实部署的跨越，为无人机机载主动视觉跟踪提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking (MOT) onboard agile unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) remains challenging due to severe viewpoint jitter induced by camera ego-motion. Rapid attitude changes during flight often lead to significant target displacement across frames, causing inaccurate target association and degraded tracking performance. Existing UAV MOT methods are primarily evaluated on offline benchmarks and seldom address the practical requirements of real-world onboard deployment, including robustness to camera motion and active target following. To address these challenges, we propose JitTrack, an active onboard multi-object tracking framework that accommodates drone dynamics and camera ego-motion. Built upon a query-based transformer tracker, JitTrack introduces semantic refinement to improve the detection of emerging targets, motion-aware query rectification to compensate for target misalignment caused by viewpoint jitter, and a motion-inspired denoising training strategy that simulates camera motion patterns for robust supervision. Furthermore, we develop a perception-planning-control closed-loop tracking pipeline for real-world deployment, enabling collision-free and physically feasible target following on agile UAVs. Extensive experiments on public UAV MOT benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over the baseline method, while real-world flight experiments validate the effectiveness and practicality of JitTrack for robust onboard visual tracking under viewpoint jitter.

</details>


### [129] [Nonlinear Model Predictive Control via Sequential Convex Programming for Drone-to-Drone Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10542)
*Neeraj Balachandar,Shriram Hari,Vishnu R. Unni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对多旋翼飞行器在扰动驱动的目标运动下进行自主空中对接问题，将其建模为有限时域最优控制问题，采用序列凸规划（SCP）结合滚动时域框架求解，并在MuJoCo高保真仿真环境中验证，在静态和匀速目标场景下实现了满足几何捕获约束的可靠对接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空中对接在扰动驱动的目标运动下属于受约束的非线性轨迹优化问题，现有方法难以同时处理动力学约束、干扰估计与实时轨迹生成，亟需一种鲁棒且计算可行的求解框架。

Method: 将对接任务建模为基于降阶非线性模型的有限时域最优控制问题，引入扰动状态扩充模型；采用序列凸规划（SCP）在滚动时域框架内求解，生成动态可行的对接轨迹；结合含噪声测量的状态估计实现鲁棒的相对运动预测，并在MuJoCo高保真刚体仿真中验证。

Result: 在静态和匀速目标场景下均能可靠收敛至对接接口，满足几何捕获约束；对接锥体违规可忽略，终端状态误差在预设容差内；锥体半角低至10度时仍保持一致且安全的对接性能；风扰标准差达0.5时仍保持鲁棒运行，且对接速度有界、控制力稳定。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于SCP的轨迹优化框架在估计不确定性和外部扰动下，对空中对接任务具有有效性，能够生成满足安全约束且鲁棒的高质量对接轨迹。

Abstract: Autonomous mid-air docking of multi-rotor vehicles under disturbance-driven target motion poses a constrained non-linear trajectory optimization challenge. This work formulates the docking task as a finite-horizon optimal control problem based on a reduced-order nonlinear model augmented with disturbance states. The resulting problem is solved using sequential convex programming within a receding-horizon framework to generate dynamically feasible docking trajectories. State estimation with noisy measurements is incorporated to enable robust relative motion prediction, while trajectory execution is validated in a high-fidelity rigid-body MuJoCo simulation environment. The proposed framework is evaluated for stationary and constant-velocity target motions, demonstrating reliable convergence to the docking interface while satisfying geometric capture constraints. Quantitatively, the method maintains negligible docking-cone violations and terminal state errors within prescribed tolerances, and achieves consistent, safe docking performance for cone half-angles as low as 10 degrees. Robust operation is observed for wind disturbance levels up to a standard deviation of 0.5, while preserving bounded approach velocities and stable control effort. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the SCP-based trajectory optimization framework for disturbance-robust aerial docking under estimation uncertainty.

</details>


### [130] [BooST: Bridging Semantics and Motions for Efficient Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10600)
*Jusuk Lee,Daesol Cho,Jonghun Shin,Seungyeon Yoo,Jonghae Park,Taekbeom Lee,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BooST，一个两阶段框架，通过跨模态VQ-VAE统一捕捉语义意图和运动动态，再将表示蒸馏为轻量级策略，同时满足技能泛化、鲁棒性和部署效率三个要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人技能学习方法要么只捕捉高层语义意图（what）、要么只捕捉底层运动动态（how），导致技能迁移不完整，产生较弱的先验知识，需要大量域内数据才能完成下游适应，无法同时满足跨任务/跨域泛化、动态扰动鲁棒性和部署效率三大需求。

Method: BooST采用两阶段框架：第一阶段利用跨模态VQ-VAE同时编码语义意图和运动动态，形成统一的技能表示；第二阶段将该表示蒸馏为轻量级策略，以实现高效的下游任务适应。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人环境中，BooST在少样本适应、跨域技能迁移以及对动态视觉干扰的鲁棒性方面均优于现有方法，同时保持了轻量且表达能力强的设计，适用于实际部署。

Conclusion: BooST通过显式桥接语义与运动，成功同时满足技能泛化、鲁棒性和效率三个目标，为机器人技能的有效迁移和实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Skill abstraction---the process of learning reusable and temporally extended behaviors---has emerged as a key paradigm for improving sample efficiency and generalization in robot learning. For efficient skill transfer to real robots, learned skills must generalize across tasks and domains, remain robust to visual and dynamic perturbations, and be efficient enough for practical deployment. However, existing methods typically satisfy only a subset of these properties, as they capture either high-level semantic intent (what) or low-level motion dynamics (how). This incomplete skill transfer yields weak priors for policy learning, thereby demanding substantial in-domain data for downstream adaptation. To address these challenges, we introduce BooST, a two-stage framework that explicitly bridges semantics and motions to satisfy all three desiderata. BooST first leverages a cross-modal VQ-VAE to capture both semantic intent and motion dynamics, yielding a unified skill representation. It then distills this representation into a lightweight policy for efficient downstream adaptation to new tasks. Extensive experiments across simulation and real-robot settings demonstrate that BooST achieves superior few-shot adaptation, cross-domain skill transfer, and robustness to dynamic visual distractors, while maintaining a lightweight yet expressive design suitable for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [131] [Toward the Cognitive--Physical Limits of Embodied Intelligence through a World-Model-Centric Autonomous Racing Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10618)
*Zitong Shan,Baichuan Lou,Yanxin Zhou,Shuge Wu,Xianqi He,Bolin Zhao,Sheng Zhao,Zhouheng Li,Chee Kiong Ong,King Ho Holden Li,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以世界模型为中心的自动驾驶赛车智能体，通过从接近极限的成功与失败中学习预测性世界模型，联合建模认知与物理极限，在真实车辆数据（最高256.3 km/h）和全尺寸仿真中实现了88.3%的交互成功率，并通过闭环细化持续提升极限利用率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数具身智能系统在保守安全边界或中等交互条件下评估，对极端环境下的能力边界理解不足。现有自动驾驶赛车系统虽然追求高速性能，但很少联合建模和细化认知与物理极限，缺乏同时处理高频率定位感知、对抗性交互、饱和车辆动力学与严格安全约束的系统性方法。

Method: 该框架从接近极限的成功与失败中学习预测性世界模型，捕捉交互演变、自车动力学和可行运动边界。方法耦合了世界状态构建、未来感知推理和近极限控制，形成闭环细化流程。训练数据来自真实车辆自动驾驶赛车（最高速度256.3 km/h，峰值横向加速度26.8 m/s²）。

Result: 在全尺寸仿真赛车的各种挑战性场景中，训练良好的世界模型中心智能体实现了88.3%的交互成功率。世界模型与策略的闭环细化进一步提升了认知-物理极限的利用、失败模式恢复能力，以及在不同条件和未见赛道上的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种边界感知方法论，其中世界模型帮助具身智能体表示、预测并持续细化其能力边界，为更安全的现实世界部署提供了可行路径。自动驾驶赛车被验证为测试具身智能认知与物理耦合极限的有效试验平台。

Abstract: Embodied artificial intelligence aims to develop agents that perceive, reason, and act through continuous interaction with the physical world. However, most embodied systems are still evaluated within conservative safety margins or moderate interaction regimes, leaving their capability boundaries under extreme conditions insufficiently understood. Autonomous racing provides a stringent testbed by combining high-frequency localization and perception, adversarial interaction, near-saturated vehicle dynamics, and strict safety constraints. Existing systems push high-speed performance but rarely model and refine cognitive and physical limits jointly. Here we show that a world-model-centric autonomous racing agent provides a concrete step toward exploring these coupled limits. The framework learns predictive world models from near-limit successes and failures to capture interaction evolution, ego dynamics, and feasible-motion boundaries, coupling world-state construction, future-aware reasoning, and near-limit control in a closed-loop refinement process. Training data were collected from real-vehicle autonomous racing, where the onboard system maintained robust localization and perception at speeds up to 256.3 km/h and peak lateral acceleration of 26.8 m/s$^2$. In full-scale simulated racing, the well trained world-model-centric agent achieves an 88.3% interaction success rate across various challenging simulated racing scenarios. Closed-loop refinement of the world model and policy further improved utilization of cognitive-physical limits, recovery from failure modes, and generalization across varying conditions and unseen circuits. These results suggest a boundary-aware methodology in which world models help embodied agents represent, predict, and continually refine their capability boundaries for safer real-world deployment.

</details>


### [132] [When Your State Estimator Has Lost The Plot: Detecting Estimator Failures Via Spectral Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10623)
*Christian Lanegger,Helen Oleynikova,Roland Siegwart,Michael Pantic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与传感器无关的频域自省方法，通过分析速度估计的频域功率分布来评估状态估计器的健康状况，在视觉-惯性、LiDAR-惯性和雷达-惯性三种里程计框架上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有状态估计器在遭遇未建模扰动（如传感器混叠或分布外噪声）时可能退化甚至失败，但缺少有效的自省机制来评估估计质量。传统协方差等不确定度量依赖理想化假设且过于自信，数据驱动方法则受限于训练数据分布。

Method: 提出一种传感器无关的频域自省方法，通过分析近期速度估计的频域功率分布（包括信号功率、谱带宽和熵等指标）来评估估计器健康状况。利用来自空中机器人的户外飞行数据进行验证，涵盖视觉-惯性、LiDAR-惯性和雷达-惯性里程计三种框架，包含多次估计器失败事件。

Result: 健康与退化估计之间存在一致的频谱功率差异，在三种不同的状态估计框架中能检测出51%-58%的标记失败，精确度为60%-84%。

Conclusion: 即使简单的频域分析也能作为轻量级的自省工具，补充现有的鲁棒性技术，为真实机器人部署提供估计质量的评估手段，并为未来研究开辟了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Reliable onboard state estimation is essential for safe robotic operation, yet unmodeled disturbances, such as sensor aliasing or out-of-distribution noise, still cause estimators to degrade or fail completely. While many methods aim to improve estimator robustness, only a few provide introspective mechanisms to assess estimate quality. Existing uncertainty measures, such as covariances, rely on idealized assumptions and tend to be overconfident, and more recent data-driven approaches are typically tied to their training data distributions.
  We propose a sensor-agnostic introspective method that assesses estimator health by analyzing the frequency-domain power distribution of recent velocity estimates. The method is evaluated using outdoor flight data from an aerial robot running visual-inertial, LiDAR-inertial, and radar-inertial odometry. The dataset includes multiple estimator failures, enabling analysis of several frequency-domain indicators, such as signal power, spectral bandwidth, and entropy. We observe consistent spectral power differences between healthy and degraded estimates, allowing detection of 51%-58% of labeled failures with 60%-84% precision across three fundamentally different state estimation frameworks. Our results show that even a simple frequency-domain analysis of a state estimator's output can serve as a lightweight introspective tool to complement existing robustness techniques in real-world robotic deployments, and opens promising avenues for future investigation.

</details>


### [133] [OAA: Three Phases of Vocal Guidance in Human-Drone Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10651)
*Allan Henry,Christian Graff,Solange Rossato,José-Ernesto Gomez-Balderas,Sylvain Huet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过两项实验（人-人手势引导和无人机遥控）发现，自发语音指导在任务推进中稳定地组织为三个阶段：方向定位（Orientation）、接近（Approach）和调整（Adjustment），可通过3D轨迹变点检测自动识别，并经Kruskal-Wallis检验验证（p<.001）。三个词汇家族在两种配置中一致复现，且停顿和词汇线索比语速更能标记阶段边界。该三阶段结构在截然不同的运动接口下均出现，说明它是人类空间引导的内在属性，而非实验配置的产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音遥控机器人系统将口头指令视为平稳信息流，忽略了引导者在任务推进过程中交际行为的变化。作者旨在揭示语音引导的阶段性动态结构，为自适应控制提供理论依据。

Method: 采集两种实验配置下（手指指示的人-人引导N=10组、游戏手柄的人-无人机遥控N=29组）的动作捕捉与语音数据，利用3D轨迹信号上的变点检测自动识别引导阶段，并通过Kruskal-Wallis检验进行统计验证，同时分析词汇家族与停顿特征。

Result: 两类实验配置中均稳定识别出Orientation、Approach、Adjustment三个阶段；旋转词汇标记Orientation、平移词汇在该阶段稀少、衰减词在Adjustment累积；语速无法标记阶段边界，但停顿与词汇线索可以。三阶段结构在两种接口下一致出现。

Conclusion: 三阶段语音引导结构是人类空间引导的内在属性，而非实验装置的产物。该发现为OAA感知的自适应控制在语音遥控遥操作应用中的实现提供了依据。

Abstract: Voice-guided teleoperation requires systems that adapt to the evolving dynamics of human guidance. Yet most voice-controlled robot systems treat spoken commands as a stationary stream, ignoring how the guide's communicative behavior changes as the task progresses. Using motion capture and speech data from two experimental configurations, humanhuman guidance (finger pointing, N =10 dyads) and humandrone teleoperation (gamepad control, N =29 dyads), we show that spontaneous vocal guidance consistently organizes into three kinematically and linguistically distinct phases: Orientation, Approach, and Adjustment. These phases are identified automatically via change point detection on 3D trajectory signals, and validated statistically (Kruskal-Wallis, p<.001). Three lexical families replicate across configurations: rotation vocabulary marks Orientation, translation vocabulary is scarce there, and attenuators accumulate toward Adjustment. Together with inter-utterance silence, these cues mark the Orientation boundary that speech rate alone leaves unmarked. The same three-phase structure emerges in both configurations despite radically different motor interfaces, suggesting it is an intrinsic property of human spatial guidance rather than an artifact of the experimental setup. We discuss implications for OAA-aware adaptive control in voice-guided teleoperation.

</details>


### [134] [Robust Sliding Mode and Admittance Control of Underactuated Aerial Manipulators for Contact-Based Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10656)
*Tareq Aziz Alqutami,Yvan Petillot,Matthew W. Dunnigan,Mustafa Suphi Erden*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于积分增强滑模控制（SMC）与导纳控制相结合的鲁棒控制框架，用于搭载1自由度机械臂的欠驱动六旋翼无人机，实现持续接触式工业检测。仿真验证表明该方法在轨迹跟踪和接触力调节方面优于传统PID控制，力调节RMSE为0.12N，可稳定施加高达20N的接触力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触式工业检测需要空中平台在保持稳定交互的同时抑制外部扰动，而欠驱动空中机械臂由于飞行器姿态与力生成之间的动态耦合，给控制带来了显著挑战。

Method: 提出一个鲁棒控制框架，由两部分组成：1）积分增强滑模控制（SMC）用于轨迹跟踪；2）导纳控制律用于力调节。接触力被映射为前馈姿态项，1自由度机械臂主动补偿倾斜以保持表面对齐。

Result: 软件在环仿真表明：SMC方法在轨迹跟踪和耦合抑制方面优于传统PID；交互策略实现了精确的力调节，RMSE为0.12N，并且能够稳定施加高达20N的力。

Conclusion: 该控制框架融合了SMC的鲁棒性与导纳控制的柔顺交互能力，有效解决了欠驱动空中操纵器的接触检测难题，验证了系统在稳定可靠接触式检测中的有效性。

Abstract: Contact-based industrial inspection requires aerial platforms to maintain stable interaction while rejecting disturbances. Underactuated aerial manipulators present control challenges due to the dynamic coupling between vehicle attitude and force generation. This paper proposes a robust control framework for an underactuated hexarotor equipped with a 1-DoF manipulator to perform sustained contact inspection. The architecture integrates integral-augmented Sliding Mode Control (SMC) for trajectory tracking with an admittance control law for force regulation. The contact force is mapped to a feedforward attitude term, while the 1-DoF arm actively compensates for the tilt to maintain surface alignment. Software-in-the-loop simulations demonstrate that the SMC-based approach achieves superior tracking and coupling rejection compared to traditional PID. Furthermore, the interaction strategy achieved precise force regulation with an RMSE of 0.12N and was able to stably exert up to 20N force, confirming the system's efficacy for stable, reliable contact-based inspection.

</details>


### [135] [TCAM for Autonomous Deformable Manipulation: The RMC2 Champion System for WBCD 2026 Track 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10718)
*Guangrui Shen,Zhili He,Shigang Wang,Yuanjun Sun,Qing Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了RMC2团队在WBCD 2026 Track 4可变形操作挑战赛中的冠军方案。该系统基于TCAM（TermiBrain因果动作模型）框架，通过定制3D打印夹爪、四摄像头视觉系统、便携式UMI演示与真实机器人演示相结合的数据收集策略，以及闭环的因果分析与策略微调机制，实现了T恤的完全自主拾取、装载、对齐和平整操作，最终以平均约23秒/件的速度装载25件T恤，其中22件达到表面平滑要求，获得Track 4第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比赛强烈激励完全自主执行，而可变形操作（如T恤的分离、运输、定位和表面调整）涉及单层分离、可变形运输、精确放置和接触丰富的表面调整等复杂物理交互，传统方法难以应对，因此需要开发一个自主解决方案。

Method: 该方案围绕TCAM框架构建全自主系统，核心理念是让硬件、感知、数据和学习的联合设计共同降低策略需要处理的物理交互复杂度。具体包括：1）定制3D打印夹爪实现单层布料分离；2）在ARX X5双机械臂平台上配置四摄像头系统（上方鱼眼相机用于任务级上下文，下方RGB相机用于近距离夹爪-布料接触观察）；3）结合便携式UMI演示和部署平台上的真实机器人演示数据；4）TCAM通过分析每个轨迹的物理因素闭环驱动数据收集和策略微调；5）策略从多视图VLA骨干输出30步末端执行器delta位姿动作块。

Result: 在最终比赛中，该系统以平均约23秒/次的速度装载了25件T恤，其中22件达到要求的表面平滑度，在Track 4中荣获第一名。

Conclusion: 通过硬件、感知、数据和学习的协同设计有效降低了可变形操作任务的物理交互复杂度，验证了TCAM框架在可变形操作领域的实用性，为类似挑战提供了可参考的工程范式。

Abstract: This technical report describes the RMC2 Team's champion solution for the WBCD 2026 Track 4: Deformable Manipulation Challenge. The task requires a robot to pick a single T-shirt from a stack, load it onto a printing pallet, align the collar with a target area, and smooth the printing region, a sequence that involves single-layer separation, deformable transport, precise placement, and contact-rich surface adjustment. The competition strongly incentivizes fully autonomous execution, motivating the development of an autonomous solution. We built a fully autonomous system around the TCAM (TermiBrain Causal Action Model) framework, with the design principle that hardware, perception, data, and learning should jointly reduce the physical interaction complexity the policy must handle. A custom 3D-printed gripper designed for single-layer fabric separation improves picking reliability on a dual-arm ARX X5 platform. A wrist-centric four-camera setup pairs upper fisheye cameras for task-level context with lower RGB cameras for close-range gripper-cloth contact observation. We combine portable UMI-style demonstrations with real-robot demonstrations collected on the deployable platform to provide both broad manipulation priors and deployment-specific dynamics. TCAM ties these components into a closed loop: each trajectory is analyzed to identify the physical factors contributing to its outcome, driving targeted data recollection and policy fine-tuning. The policy outputs 30-step end-effector delta-pose action chunks from a multi-view VLA backbone. In the final competition, our system loaded 25 T-shirts at an average of approximately 23 seconds per attempt, with 22 achieving the required surface smoothness, securing first place in Track 4.

</details>


### [136] [Embodied Multimodal Grounding for Open-Vocabulary Mobile Manipulation via Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10756)
*Huosen Ou,Dongni Song,Yuncong Wang,Tao Zhou,Yiding Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具身多模态接地框架，通过主动多视角语义3D高斯泼溅（Semantic-3DGS）、可达性感知的基座定位和基于扩散的视觉-语言-动作策略，实现了开放词汇目标接地与少样本操作。系统在50次真实机器人实验中，长时程任务成功率60%，远超对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身移动操作要求语言、视觉观测、三维场景结构和动作可行性在执行前对齐，但现有方法在杂乱环境、遮挡、视角变化和具身约束下鲁棒性不足，亟需统一的3D语义接地框架来弥合感知与动作之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出任务驱动的局部Semantic-3DGS作为共享接口，统一主动感知、语言条件3D定位、障碍物感知场景推理、基座预备和动作模型语义条件化。通过仅向后期动作专家模块注入3D语义线索来保留预训练动作先验，并集成可达性感知的基座定位和扩散策略。

Result: 在50次真实机器人评估中，完整系统长时程任务成功率60%（PointVLA 40%，DexVLA 28%）；重度杂乱操作成功率74%（单视角变体52%，PointVLA 46%）；75厘米高度偏移下仍保持75%成功率，并消除了照片诱导的虚假抓取。

Conclusion: 显式、可刷新的3D语义接地能显著提升杂乱环境、遮挡、视角变化和具身约束下的操作鲁棒性，验证了将3D语义信息作为共享接口的有效性。

Abstract: Embodied mobile manipulation requires language, visual observations, three-dimensional scene structure, and action feasibility to be aligned before execution. We study open-vocabulary target grounding with few-shot manipulation in local household workspaces and present an embodied multimodal grounding framework that integrates active multi-view Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (Semantic-3DGS), reachability-aware base positioning, and a diffusion-based vision-language-action policy. A task-driven local Semantic-3DGS serves as a shared interface across active sensing, language-conditioned 3D localization, obstacle-aware scene reasoning, base preparation, and semantic conditioning of the action model. To preserve pretrained action priors, the 3D semantic cues are injected only into the late action-expert blocks. In expanded 50-trial real-robot evaluations against representative vision-language-action (VLA) approaches, the full system achieves 60% long-horizon success compared with 40% for PointVLA and 28% for DexVLA, and reaches 74% success in heavily cluttered manipulation compared with 52% for the single-view variant and 46% for PointVLA. It also maintains 75% success under a 75 cm height shift and eliminates photo-induced false grasps. These results indicate that explicit, refreshable 3D semantic grounding can improve robustness under clutter, occlusion, viewpoint variation, and embodiment constraints.

</details>


### [137] [JEPA-WAM: Stage-Level Joint-Embedding Prediction for World-Action Models in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10780)
*Xiao Liu,Yuguang Yang,Xi Wang,Kai Jiang,Cheng Chi,Yong Xu,Wenchao Ding,Yilun Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出JEPA-WAM，将短期的物理未来预测与长期的阶段级语义未来预测相结合，通过Stage-JEPA预测下一任务阶段的潜在目标，在50个RoboTwin 2.0任务中达到90.25%的成功率，并将执行步数减少5.97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用机器人策略通常将未来表示为固定的短时视频-动作片段，只能捕捉局部场景演变，无法明确描述任务应从当前阶段推进到下一阶段的阶段级未来。这限制了任务执行过程中的长期规划和语义理解能力。

Method: 提出JEPA-WAM框架，包含两个互补的未来预测模块：(1) 基于Motus的世界动作模型（WAM）负责短期的物理未来预测；(2) 新增的Stage-JEPA模块采用目标条件的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），利用冻结的V-JEPA2编码器提取当前状态表示，并预测下一推断阶段的潜在目标表示。

Result: 在50个RoboTwin 2.0任务（包括干净环境和随机化环境）中，JEPA-WAM实现了90.25%的整体成功率，相比最强基线将成功回放中的平均执行步数减少了5.97%。

Conclusion: 将阶段级语义未来预测与短期物理未来预测相结合，能够显著提升通用机器人策略的任务完成率与执行效率，验证了"双未来"建模在机器人操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies aim to map multimodal observations and linguistic task instructions to actions across diverse tasks. However, existing methods typically represent the future as a fixed, short video-action chunk. This short-term future captures local scene evolution for action execution, but it does not explicitly describe the stage-level future that specifies how a task should progress from its current stage to the next. We therefore distinguish two complementary futures for robot manipulation: a short-term physical future to capture local scene evolution and a stage-level semantic future to represent task progress. We introduce JEPA-WAM, which augments a Motus-based World Action Model (WAM) with Stage-JEPA, a goal-conditioned Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) predictor. Given the current observation and task instruction, Stage-JEPA uses a frozen V-JEPA2 encoder to extract the current-state representation and predicts the latent target of the next inferred stage. Across 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks in clean and randomized environments, JEPA-WAM achieves 90.25% overall success and reduces the mean number of execution steps in successful rollouts by 5.97% relative to the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [138] [Dual Stress: Runtime Safety Monitoring for Safety-Constrained MPC Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10791)
*Jamil Chahine,Wenqi Cai,John Abanes,Anthony Tzes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用模型预测控制器中KKT乘子的加权和作为"对偶应力"信号，与几何危险监测器互补，在相同虚警预算下可多标记4.7倍被几何方法遗漏的碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动驾驶危险监测器仅依赖几何量（间隙、碰撞时间、所需减速度），忽略了模型预测控制器每个控制步中计算出的KKT乘子这一额外信息通道，该乘子能衡量为维持安全所消耗的边际控制代价。

Method: 将KKT乘子的水平加权和定义为"对偶应力"信号，与15个几何检测器进行对比，在预注册的留出交叉场景中通过物理仿真器驱动测试，并匹配相同虚警预算。

Result: 应力警报标记了85个被整个几何检测器组遗漏的碰撞，而几何检测器仅标记了18个应力遗漏的碰撞（比率4.7:1）；两者结合可预警四分之三的制动仍可行的碰撞，而几何检测器单独仅能预警不到一半。

Conclusion: 对偶应力信号是与几何警示互补的有效危险监测通道，两者结合能显著提升自动驾驶系统的碰撞预警能力。

Abstract: Runtime hazard monitors for autonomous naviga- tion are conventionally built from geometric quantities: predicted clearance, time to collision, and required deceleration. A model-predictive controller that enforces safety through explicit con- straints computes, as a by-product of every control step, a second information channel that such monitors ignore: the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker multipliers of its constrained optimization, which measure the marginal control effort spent to maintain safety against each obstacle. This paper evaluates whether a horizon-weighted sum of those multipliers, a dual stress signal, provides a hazard monitor complementary to the geometric warnings the same state already supports. We compare it against a battery of fifteen geometric detectors tuned to a matched false-alarm budget, on preregistered held-out crossing scenarios driven through a physics simulator. The stress alarm actionably flags 4.7 times as many collisions missed by the entire geometric battery as the geometric battery flags in return (85 versus 18); combined, the two channels warn of three quarters of the collisions for which braking remained feasible, against under half for the geometric battery alone.

</details>


### [139] [AECNav: Active Evidence Consolidation for Efficient Zero-Shot Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10817)
*Guanlin Liu,Shaobin Ling,Renyuan Liu,Zeying Gong,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AECNav，一种无需训练的零样本目标导航流水线，通过证据门控感知、证据整合和主动证据获取三个模块，重构了感知到决策的证据驱动过程，在HM3D-v2、HM3D-OVON和MP3D基准上分别达到84.7%、57.3%和51.3%的成功率，并在实体四足机器人上以约5Hz的频率实现95%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本目标导航（ZSON）方法存在高延迟和有限准确率两大问题，主要源于冗余的感知管线以及缺乏可靠的证据来确认目标是否被找到。作者希望解决这两个瓶颈，提升系统效率和决策可靠性。

Method: AECNav包含三个核心组件：1) 证据门控感知——在所有推理阶段共享编码器以建立统一语义基础并消除冗余计算；2) 证据整合——将检测结果聚合并转换为簇级对数几率信念，区分真实目标支持与视觉相似干扰物的虚假置信度，并将预期检测缺失作为负面证据处理；3) 主动证据获取——在语义线索较弱时，通过选择信息增益最大且遍历成本最小的前沿来维持高效探索。

Result: AECNav在HM3D-v2、HM3D-OVON和MP3D数据集上分别达到84.7%、57.3%和51.3%的成功率，显著优于此前方法，同时计算开销大幅降低；在实体四足机器人上进行40次试验，以约5Hz的频率达到95%的成功率。

Conclusion: 通过将ZSON重新定义为证据驱动的感知到决策问题，AECNav证明了共享语义编码、证据积累和主动探索策略的有效结合可以在显著降低计算开销的同时提升导航成功率，并展示了在真实机器人平台上部署的可行性。

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation (ZSON) in open-vocabulary scenarios is challenging, as it requires a robot to locate an arbitrarily specified object in an unseen environment without task-specific training. Currently, the task still suffers from high latency and limited accuracy due to redundant perception pipelines and insufficient evidence for reliable target confirmation. In this letter, we reframe ZSON as an evidence-driven perception-to-decision problem and present AECNav, a training-free pipeline built on three components: i) Evidence-gated perception, which utilizes a shared encoding across all reasoning stages to establish a unified semantic basis and eliminate redundant computations; ii) Evidence consolidation, which aggregates detections into cluster-level log-odds beliefs. This explicitly separates genuine target support from the false confidence of visually similar distractors, while treating the absence of expected detections as negative evidence; and iii) Active evidence acquisition, which sustains productive exploration under weak semantic cues by selecting frontiers that maximize information gain at minimal traversal cost.
